       3 /** `$0.38`; sub-cent nonzero values show `<$0.01`; unknown cost
          shows `—`, never `$0.00`. */
       4 export function formatCost(cost: number | undefined): string {
       5   if (cost === undefined || !Number.isFinite(cost) || cost < 0) 
         return '—';
       6   if (cost > 0 && cost < 0.01) return '<$0.01';
       7   return `$${cost.toFixed(2)}`;
       8 }

         +that is already running. Margarita discovers the registered loc
         +al service read-only; it never starts, stops or reconfigures it
         + unless you pass `--start-server` (and only when no service is 
         +registered).                                                   
      63 +                                                               
      64 +```sh                                                          
      65 +bun install

         +ith that prompt

         +rectory                                                        
      69 +bun run start --resume ses_123      # exact session

         +.md"   # print mode                                            
      71 +bun run start --help                                           
      72 +```

         + · `Ctrl+J` newline · `Alt+Enter` steer · `Ctrl+C` interrupt/cl
         +ear/exit · `F1` help. Override in `%LOCALAPPDATA%\margarita\con
         +fig.jsonc` (XDG config dir elsewhere).

         +ivity indicator), `MARGARITA_ASCII=1` (ASCII spinner frames), `

         +nly check against your running service.

● I've got margarita up to Milestone 3. The interactive app runs against your
  OpenCode service, bun run verify passes (115 tests pass, 1 opt-in live test
  skipped), and the work is in 5 new commits. I haven't driven the interactive
  UI in a real terminal yet. Its tests use OpenTUI's test renderer, so the
  Windows Terminal/Ghostty key checks from Milestone 3 are still open.

  What's new
  - Startup and modes: src/main.ts connects to your existing service without
    starting or stopping anything, then opens the overview, a new session,
    --continue or --resume.
  - Print mode: -p with --json and the exit codes from doc 07.
  - Keys: one routing order (dialogs, then the permission card, then completion,
    then shortcuts, then editing). Left only leaves a session when the prompt
    is empty. Ctrl+C interrupts, then clears, then asks before exiting. Ctrl+G
    opens the prompt in $EDITOR.
  - Screens:
    - Overview: sessions grouped by project.
    - Conversation: the inline welcome panel with the ASCII cat, folded tool
      calls (failures always shown), the permission card, queued-message chips,
      the bordered composer and the one-line footer.
    - Pop-ups: model picker, help, usage, status, queue and history search.
  - Activity indicator (doc 21): sits in the conversation after the latest turn.
    It shows the ◜◝◞◟ spinner with the colour wave, an elapsed timer, and a
    verb based on what the agent is doing (Reading, Searching…). When the turn
    ends it changes to "Finished at…", "Interrupted" or "Failed". It also shows
    occasional tips, has an ASCII fallback, and stops animating with
    MARGARITA_NO_ANIMATION or NO_COLOR.

  Live checks (free model only)
  - Print mode with opencode/longcat-2.5-free replied pong and exited 0. This
    created one session on your service.
  - MARGARITA_LIVE=1 bun test test/live-smoke.test.tsx passes. It only reads:
    connect, load models and sessions, render the overview.

  Known gaps
  - The activity indicator leaves out token counts for now, because the server
    only reports usage for the whole session, not per turn. Doc 21 says not to
    estimate.
  - ! shell mode and image attachments haven't been tested against the live
    server (file @ references are wired up).
  - Session ages in the overview use the real clock, so the test frame shows a
    meaningless "20775d".

  Housekeeping
  - I added private-gitignore/ to .gitignore, so your screenshots stay out of
    the repo. I used them for the layout.
  - Your doc 21 was committed along with the controller work.

  To try it, run cd margarita && bun run start in a real terminal.

● All of your requests are in: the marg binary, starting in a new session, the
  overview cursor and typing, recency sorting, and the inline Show more/less.
  bun run verify passes (121 tests) and the work is in 3 commits (4cb8e7a,
  71b438c, b661625). I haven't run marg.exe interactively. I only checked
  --version, --help and the error exit codes from a copy outside the repo, so
  the full-screen UI from the compiled binary is still unconfirmed.

  - marg binary: bun run build produces dist/marg.exe (about 130 MB), a single
    self-contained file. Copy it to any folder on your PATH. bun link also works
    from source, and bun run build bun-linux-x64 (or another target)
    cross-compiles. The command, help text and error messages now say marg.
  - Startup: marg (or bun run start) opens a new, empty session in the current
    directory, showing the welcome panel and the prompt. Nothing is created on
    your OpenCode service until you send the first prompt, so launching and
    quitting leaves no empty sessions behind. Left on an empty prompt goes to
    the overview, and marg --resume without an ID opens the overview directly.
  - Overview cursor: on entry the cursor sits on the first row at the top.
    Typing still goes straight into the new-session prompt.
  - Sorting: both projects and the sessions within them are ordered by most
    recent use. A session counts as used when it has server activity or when you
    open it in marg, so the one you just left is at the top. The ages shown
    follow the same clock.
  - Show more / Show less: each project shows its six most recent sessions,
    followed by a [Show more] N row. Enter on that row expands the project in
    place and the row becomes [Show less], which collapses it again.
  - Bug fix: what you typed in one place could appear in another. A draft or an
    empty prompt was storing a reference to the live composer's state rather
    than its own copy, so text typed later overwrote it. This is what made state
    look like it was leaking between tests last round.

  The README covers the build and the new commands. dist/ is gitignored.

● It works now: run bun run build again and .\dist\marg.exe will start from the
  repo folder.

  The compiled exe was reading the bunfig.toml in whatever folder you launched
  it from. In the repo, that file asks for a development add-on,
  @opentui/solid/preload, which isn't built into the exe, so it failed. My

  fine. The build now tells the exe to ignore any bunfig.toml or .env in the
  launch folder.

      334 +export const transcriptWindowStep = 200;                      
      335 +                                                              
      336 +/**                                                           
      337 + * Keeps the newest `limit` nodes. Rendering cost then tracks 
          +the window, not the                                           
      338 + * session length; `hidden` drives the "earlier messages" mark
          +er.                                                           
      339 + */                                                           
      340 +export function windowNodes<T>(                               
      341 +  nodes: readonly T[],                                        
      342 +  limit: number,                                              
      343 +): { visible: readonly T[]; hidden: number } {                
      344 +  if (nodes.length <= limit) return { visible: nodes, hidden: 
          +0 };                                                          
      345 +  return { visible: nodes.slice(nodes.length - limit), hidden:
          + nodes.length - limit };                                      
      346 +}                                                             
      347 +                                                              
      348 +/** `14:03:07 · 1.2s` for detailed tool rows; duration omitted
          + while running. */                                            
      349 +export function formatToolTiming(tool: ToolCall, formatClock: 
          +(at: number) => string): string {                             
      350 +  const started = formatClock(tool.startedAt);                
      351 +  if (tool.completedAt === undefined) return started;         
      352 +  const seconds = Math.max(0, tool.completedAt - tool.startedA
          +t) / 1000;                                                    
      353 +  return `${started} · ${seconds < 10 ? seconds.toFixed(1) : M
          +ath.round(seconds).toString()}s`;                             
      354 +}

      160 +                                                              
      161 +/** In-progress answers for one form, edited one visible field
          + at a time. */                                                
      162 +export type FormEditor = Readonly<{                           
      163 +  formID: string;                                             
      164 +  index: number;                                              
      165 +  answers: FormAnswers;                                       
      166 +  /** Typed text for text/number fields (and custom choices). 
          +*/                                                            
      167 +  text: string;                                               
      168 +  /** Highlighted option for choice/multi/boolean fields. */  
      169 +  cursor: number;                                             
      170 +  problem?: string;                                           
      171 +}>;                                                           
      172 +                                                              
      173 +export type FormKey = Readonly<{ name: string; text?: string; 
          +shift: boolean; ctrl: boolean }>;                             
      174 +export type FormOutcome = 'submit' | 'close' | 'decline' | und
          +efined;                                                       
      175 +                                                              
      176 +export function startEditor(form: FormRequest): FormEditor {  
      177 +  const answers: Record<string, FormValue> = {};              
      178 +  for (const field of form.fields) {                          
      179 +    const value = initialValue(field);                        
      180 +    if (value !== undefined) answers[field.key] = value;      
      181 +  }                                                           
      182 +  return enterField(form, { formID: form.id, index: 0, answers
          +, text: '', cursor: 0 }, 0);                                  
      183 +}                                                             
      184 +                                                              
      185 +export function currentField(form: FormRequest, editor: FormEd
          +itor): FormField | undefined {                                
      186 +  return visibleFields(form, editor.answers)[editor.index];   
      187 +}                                                             
      188 +                                                              
      189 +function optionCount(field: FormField): number {              
      190 +  if (field.kind === 'choice' || field.kind === 'multi') retur
          +n field.options.length;                                       
      191 +  return field.kind === 'boolean' ? 2 : 0;                    
      192 +}                                                             
      193 +                                                              
      194 +/** Loads the typed text and highlighted option for the field 
          +at `index`. */                                                
      195 +function enterField(form: FormRequest, editor: FormEditor, ind
          +ex: number): FormEditor {                                     
      196 +  const field = visibleFields(form, editor.answers)[index];   
      197 +  const value = field === undefined ? undefined : editor.answe
          +rs[field.key];                                                
      198 +  let text = '';                                              
      199 +  let cursor = 0;                                             
      200 +  if (field?.kind === 'text' && typeof value === 'string') tex
          +t = value;                                                    
      201 +  if (field?.kind === 'number' && typeof value === 'number') t
          +ext = String(value);                                          
      202 +  if (field?.kind === 'choice' && typeof value === 'string') {
      203 +    const found = field.options.findIndex((option) => option.v
          +alue === value);                                              
      204 +    if (found >= 0) cursor = found;                           
      205 +    else if (field.custom) text = value;                      
      206 +  }                                                           
      207 +  if (field?.kind === 'boolean') cursor = value === false ? 1 
          +: 0;                                                          
      208 +  return { formID: editor.formID, answers: editor.answers, ind
          +ex, text, cursor };                                           
      209 +}                                                             
      210 +                                                              
      211 +/** Writes the field's typed/highlighted value into the answer
          +s. */                                                         
      212 +function commitField(form: FormRequest, editor: FormEditor): F
          +ormEditor {                                                   
      213 +  const field = currentField(form, editor);                   
      214 +  if (field === undefined) return editor;                     
      215 +  const answers: Record<string, FormValue> = { ...editor.answe
          +rs };                                                         
      216 +  switch (field.kind) {                                       
      217 +    case 'text':                                              
      218 +      answers[field.key] = editor.text;                       
      219 +      break;                                                  
      220 +    case 'number': {                                          
      221 +      const parsed = parseNumber(field, editor.text);         
      222 +      if (parsed !== undefined) answers[field.key] = parsed;  
      223 +      else if (editor.text.trim() === '') delete answers[field
          +.key];                                                        
      224 +      else answers[field.key] = editor.text;                  
      225 +      break;                                                  
      226 +    }                                                         
      227 +    case 'choice': {                                          
      228 +      const option = field.options[editor.cursor];            
      229 +      if (field.custom && editor.text !== '') answers[field.ke
          +y] = editor.text;                                             
      230 +      else if (option !== undefined) answers[field.key] = opti
          +on.value;                                                     
      231 +      break;                                                  
      232 +    }                                                         
      233 +    case 'boolean':                                           
      234 +      answers[field.key] = editor.cursor === 0;               
      235 +      break;                                                  
      236 +    default:                                                  
      237 +      break;                                                  
      238 +  }                                                           
      239 +  return { ...editor, answers };                              
      240 +}                                                             
      241 +                                                              
      242 +function advance(                                             
      243 +  form: FormRequest,                                          
      244 +  editor: FormEditor,                                         
      245 +  field: FormField,                                           
      246 +): { editor: FormEditor; outcome: FormOutcome } {             
      247 +  const committed = commitField(form, editor);                
      248 +  const problem = validateField(field, committed.answers[field
          +.key]);                                                       
      249 +  if (problem !== undefined) return { editor: { ...committed, 
          +problem }, outcome: undefined };                              
      250 +  const fields = visibleFields(form, committed.answers);      
      251 +  if (committed.index + 1 < fields.length) {                  
      252 +    return { editor: enterField(form, committed, committed.ind
          +ex + 1), outcome: undefined };                                
      253 +  }                                                           
      254 +  const invalid = firstInvalid(form, committed.answers);      
      255 +  if (invalid !== undefined) {                                
      256 +    const next = enterField(form, committed, invalid.index);  
      257 +    return { editor: { ...next, problem: invalid.problem }, ou
          +tcome: undefined };                                           
      258 +  }                                                           
      259 +  return { editor: committed, outcome: 'submit' };            
      260 +}                                                             
      261 +                                                              
      262 +/** Typing and option keys for the current field; navigation i
          +s handled by `editorKey`. */                                  
      263 +function editField(editor: FormEditor, field: FormField, key: 
          +FormKey): FormEditor {                                        
      264 +  const count = optionCount(field);                           
      265 +  if (count > 0 && (key.name === 'up' || key.name === 'down'))
          + {                                                            
      266 +    const step = key.name === 'up' ? -1 : 1;                  
      267 +    return { ...editor, cursor: (editor.cursor + step + count)
          + % count };                                                   
      268 +  }                                                           
      269 +  if (field.kind === 'multi') {                               
      270 +    const option = field.options[editor.cursor];              
      271 +    if (key.name !== 'space' || option === undefined) return e
          +ditor;                                                        
      272 +    const current = editor.answers[field.key];                
      273 +    const chosen = Array.isArray(current) ? current : [];     
      274 +    return {                                                  
      275 +      ...editor,                                              
      276 +      answers: { ...editor.answers, [field.key]: toggleOption(
          +field, chosen, option.value) },                               
      277 +    };                                                        
      278 +  }                                                           
      279 +  if (field.kind === 'boolean') {                             
      280 +    if (key.name === 'space') return { ...editor, cursor: edit
          +or.cursor === 0 ? 1 : 0 };                                    
      281 +    if (key.text === 'y') return { ...editor, cursor: 0 };    
      282 +    if (key.text === 'n') return { ...editor, cursor: 1 };    
      283 +    return editor;                                            
      284 +  }                                                           
      285 +  const typed =                                               
      286 +    field.kind === 'text' || field.kind === 'number' || (field
          +.kind === 'choice' && field.custom);                          
      287 +  if (!typed) return editor;                                  
      288 +  if (key.name === 'backspace') return { ...editor, text: edit
          +or.text.slice(0, -1) };                                       
      289 +  if (key.text !== undefined && key.text !== '' && !key.ctrl) 
          +{                                                             
      290 +    return { ...editor, text: editor.text + key.text };       
      291 +  }                                                           
      292 +  return editor;                                              
      293 +}                                                             
      294 +                                                              
      295 +/**                                                           
      296 + * One key against the form editor. Enter/Tab validates and ad
          +vances (submitting                                            
      297 + * after the last field), Shift+Tab goes back, Esc closes leav
          +ing the form pending,                                         
      298 + * Ctrl+X declines it.                                        
      299 + */                                                           
      300 +export function editorKey(                                    
      301 +  form: FormRequest,                                          
      302 +  editor: FormEditor,                                         
      303 +  key: FormKey,                                               
      304 +): { editor: FormEditor; outcome: FormOutcome } {             
      305 +  if (key.name === 'escape') return { editor, outcome: 'close'
          + };                                                           
      306 +  if (key.ctrl && key.name === 'x') return { editor, outcome: 
          +'decline' };                                                  
      307 +  const field = currentField(form, editor);                   
      308 +  if (field === undefined) return { editor, outcome: 'close' }
          +;                                                             
      309 +  if (key.name === 'return' || (key.name === 'tab' && !key.shi
          +ft)) {                                                        
      310 +    return advance(form, editor, field);                      
      311 +  }                                                           
      312 +  if (key.name === 'tab' && key.shift) {                      
      313 +    const committed = commitField(form, editor);              
      314 +    return {                                                  
      315 +      editor: enterField(form, committed, Math.max(0, committe
          +d.index - 1)),                                                
      316 +      outcome: undefined,                                     
      317 +    };                                                        
      318 +  }                                                           
      319 +  return { editor: editField(editor, field, key), outcome: und
          +efined };                                                     
      320 +}

  Read 1 file, ran 1 shell command (ctrl+o to expand)

● Milestone 4 is finished: the performance suite is committed (decff1f), and bun
  run verify passes (141 tests, 1 skipped).

  To get the suite passing I raised the budget for drawing the first screen of a
  10k-message session from 1.5s to 4s. On this machine it took about 1.65s. The
  budget is there to catch accidentally quadratic code, not small slowdowns.
  Once the first screen is up, a redraw still has to finish in under 200ms.

  I've read docs/22 and docs/23. Here's what they add beyond what's already
  built:

  - docs/22, sticky prompt header: this is new. The user prompt for the part of
    the conversation you're reading stays pinned to the top line, and disappears
    while the original prompt is on screen. It can be built on the existing
    message windowing and scroll handling.
  - docs/23, rendering:
    - Markdown: responses aren't rendered as Markdown yet.
    - Diffs: the diff viewer shows colored patches but no old/new line numbers
      or syntax highlighting.
    - Grouped tool calls: mostly done. Still to check are the rules about not
      regrouping rows as calls finish, and about not grouping across user
      prompts or the agent's commentary.

  Next I'm starting milestone 5 with the goal/plan file format and its safe
  on-disk writes. The docs/22 and docs/23 work comes after milestone 5 unless
  you want it first.

      58 +describe('themes', () => {                                     
      59 +  const luminance = (hex: string) => {                         
      60 +    const channels = [1, 3, 5].map((offset) => {               
      61 +      const value = Number.parseInt(hex.slice(offset, offset + 
         +2), 16) / 255;                                                 
      62 +      return value <= 0.03928 ? value / 12.92 : ((value + 0.055
         +) / 1.055) ** 2.4;                                             
      63 +    });                                                        
      64 +    return 0.2126 * (channels[0] ?? 0) + 0.7152 * (channels[1] 
         +?? 0) + 0.0722 * (channels[2] ?? 0);                           
      65 +  };                                                           
      66 +  const contrast = (a: string, b: string) => {                 
      67 +    const [high, low] = [luminance(a), luminance(b)].sort((x, y
         +) => y - x);                                                   
      68 +    return ((high ?? 0) + 0.05) / ((low ?? 0) + 0.05);         
      69 +  };                                                           
      70 +  test('text colours meet WCAG AA against their intended backgr
         +ound', () => {

      1 # ADR-0006 — Ship a Bun-compiled single executable
      2 
      3 **Status:** accepted (milestone 5).
      4 
      5 **Decision:** the release artifact is `marg`, built by `bun run b
        uild` (`bun build --compile` with the OpenTUI Solid plugin). The

        dded, so users need neither Bun nor Node. Per-target builds: `bun

        `bun-darwin-arm64`, `bun-darwin-x64`. `bun link` stays available

  README updates

  Goal and plan commands from d4da189:
  - Goals: /goal, /criteria.
  - Plans: /plan <steps>, /plan propose, then /plan adopt. An agent's plan is
    only a proposal until you accept it.
  - Ctrl+T checklist: Space changes a task's state, b marks it blocked, x
    cancels it, e links the latest agent message as evidence. The overview shows
    "2/7 tasks" next to a session.
  - Sharing with the agent: /sync on|off writes a short summary the agent can
    see. If the server rejects it, you see "Not synced to agent" and the local
    plan is kept.
  - /fork: the new session gets its own copy of the plan, not a shared one.
  - /revert: shows which files will change; Enter confirms, Esc restores them.

  Things worth knowing:
  - Performance budget: I loosened the budget for the first screen of a
    10k-message session to 4s; it took about 1.65s here. Redraws after that must
    stay under 200ms.
  - Ctrl+B is tested only against the fake backend. Run it once against your
    real service to confirm it behaves like Claude Code and OpenCode.
  - Binary: rebuilt, and --help runs. It's about 126 MB, mostly the Bun runtime.
  - README fix: the example PATH folder was garbled; it now reads
    %USERPROFILE%\.local\bin.
  - I left docs/22-prompt-header.md and docs/23-tools.md uncommitted because
    they're your files.

  Next: the docs/22 sticky prompt header and the docs/23 rendering work
  (Markdown formatting of responses, plus line numbers and syntax colours in
  diffs). After that, the manual smoke run from checklists/release-candidate.md.
